Owner: "The new version of the browser loads Claude.ai." (Kestrel G on the real tablet: the first real-device success.)
Standing rules in that message: frequently checkpoint and save ALL progress to GitHub; optimize the project for MULTIPLE Claude sessions and ACCOUNTS; a resumed session has NO context and must continue from where the last one was
interrupted by usage or finished its last task; no progress/data loss, no wasted Claude usage; "Always keep my prompts and respond to each one even if I interrupt. Don't ignore any prompt."; "Send me the prompt again if I pressed
deny on accident" (a denied tool call is re-sent ONCE, say so in one line); the Brave problem - "it freezes a lot especially when I'm typing and I have to switch apps and go back to brave for it to refresh. I don't want this
problem with kestrel".
Work order: M (process) and R (research, in parallel) -> A (speed, freeze, security quick wins) -> B (downloads) -> C (UI) -> D (blocking engine v2). Every step: ckpt. Every release: ship.sh + Actions + record-release.

I don't think free space was the issue because I installed it from github fine" - CAUSE FOUND AND MEASURED (research/UPDATE_STORAGE.md): not the certificate (same on all releases); Android refuses ANY install (in place or fresh) unless 10 % of the data partition stays free afterwards, on top of the app's own room (staged APK + unpacked libs 131.7 MB + optimised code): emulator 6.2 GB partition: same APK passes at 1050 MiB free, fails at 1000, fresh install fails at the same point; installd logs "Failed to free up 624066560" = exactly 10 %. "Installed fine from GitHub" was after the uninstall freed about a gigabyte. DONE in code (alpha8, emulator-verified): UpdateLogic.storagePlan = reserve (10 %) + libs + staged APK + 64 MB, free = StorageStatsManager.getFreeBytes (counts clearable caches), Updates sheet shows the real numbers, "Delete old installers" button (the download manager's own kestrel-*.apk files), Diagnostics Storage line, release notes hint. NOT done: [ ] ask the owner for Settings > Storage total/free to confirm 10 % on the tablet, [ ] Back up / Restore (bookmarks, settings, open tabs) only if in-place updates still fail after alpha8, [ ] measure the release build's optimised-code margin (64 MB is a guess; the debug build needs about 100-150)
- [~] D1 Blocking engine inside the extension (no round trip to Kotlin per request), same lists + per-site switches, counters batched {ccr-a57c163e-mtq8js 2026-11-11T22:29Z} **CODE DONE (alpha13, emulator-verified, tablet numbers pending)**: design `research/D1-DESIGN.md`; `core/.../filter/ExtImage.kt` (KEX1 image of the loaded lists + `contentId` + `chunkReply`), `geckoapp/.../kestrel-shields/engine.js` (line-by-line JS port of `FilterSet.check`/`Psl`/`hostOf`), new `background.js` (synchronous decisions, one `cfg` question per page, chunked image transfer, saved copy in the extension's IndexedDB, batched per-category counts, `st` numbers, per-request path as start-up fallback and as the compatibility switch), `ShieldsBridge` (cfg/img/cnt/st), `Engine.snapshot()` generations, Diagnostics 'Blocker' line + switch (`Prefs.engineInExtension`). ## Owner request 8, 2026-11-11T19:15Z (verbatim in INBOX.md; the owner now runs alpha8/9 and says "The app can now update with enough storage space" = U1 confirmed on the tablet) - background downloads + notifications + swipe-to-quit + video downloader (InShot-style) + neon still invisible + speed review
Work order: alpha10 = V1-V4 + V7 (background engine, notifications, quit/sleep, neon) -> alpha11 = V5-V6 (video downloader) -> alpha12 = V8 (speed review, D1). Tick a box only with the test/run that proves it. Facts the owner must hear: Android 10 cannot install an app silently (the system "Install?" screen is mandatory); DRM video (Netflix, Disney+, Prime...) cannot be saved; YouTube forbids downloads and needs per-site extractors.
- [x] V0 "The app can now update with enough storage space." (recorded: U1 worked on the real tablet once the room was there) [recorded: the owner's own report; shipped line alpha9 in BUILDLOG]
- [x] V1 "Make the app download and download and install updates even in the background, downloads should continue at full speed even if the tablet is on the lock screen and the screen is turned off." (foreground service + partial wake lock + Wi-Fi lock held only while bytes are moving; download threads not in the background CPU group; the UPDATE download moves into the same engine/service; install: Android 10 always asks - a "Update ready, tap to install" notification carries the system confirmation; API 31+ may install without the prompt) **CODE DONE + EMULATOR-TESTED (alpha10)**: the update APK downloads through the same engine in DownloadService (`destKind` DEST_APP_PRIVATE, FileSinks/AppSinks), service holds a partial wake lock + Wi-Fi lock, download threads at normal priority whenever the UI is not visible; proof: emulator screen-off run (progress continued, parallel Range connections seen at the server), `EngineTest.theDestinationKindReachesTheSinkAndTheView`, `AppSinksTest`. NOT verified on the tablet (hotspot, doze, battery). [shipped kestrel-g-v0.2.0-alpha10, BUILDLOG: updates and downloads run in the background]
- [x] V2 "Put notifications for update status and download statuses." (update: checking/available/downloading %/ready to install/failed/installed; downloads: progress with Pause/Cancel, paused, failed with Retry, finished with Open; one grouped summary) **CODE DONE + EMULATOR-TESTED (alpha10)**: UpdateNotifier (available / downloading / ready with an Install action / failed / needs space / confirm) + per-download Pause/Cancel/Retry actions + daily ReleaseCheckJob; emulator showed 'Kestrel 9.9.9 is ready to install' with the Install action. Tap on a notification now first closes any full-screen panel (`dismissOverlays`). [shipped kestrel-g-v0.2.0-alpha10, BUILDLOG: own notifications with Pause/Cancel/Retry]
- [x] V3 "Remove the "quit" button from the menu. I will swipe to close the app to quit. If I swipe to close the app, keep downloads active, but if everything is inactive and there are no downloads, make sure the app properly sleeps and doesn't waste battery or ram or processor when it is not in use." (menu item gone; swipe-away with active downloads = downloads keep going without the UI cost, swipe-away with nothing running = the whole process exits (Gecko, threads, jobs); clear-on-exit runs on swipe-away) **CODE DONE + EMULATOR-TESTED (alpha10)**: Quit is gone from the menu; with NOTHING running a swipe from Recents ended all 4 app processes at once and none restarted (am_remove_task + am_proc_died, 2 runs); with an UPDATE DOWNLOAD running the swipe destroyed the activity, kept the process + Gecko helpers + the foreground service alive, the download carried on to the end, and the process exited by itself ~6 s after the last byte (ProcessLife). Fixed on the way: the crash-helper process outlived the main one as a cached process (~50 MB), ProcessLife now ends this app's sibling processes first. Honest limit: while a download runs after the swipe the Gecko engine and its helpers stay in memory (about 280 MB main process on the x86_64 emulator); moving downloads into a download-only process would free that (task V3b, not started). [shipped kestrel-g-v0.2.0-alpha10, BUILDLOG: Quit removed, swipe-away keeps downloads, ProcessLife exits when idle]
- [x] V4 "I use an app called video downloader by inshot to download videos from web pages. Figure out how it works and allow me to download videos from any web page online by long pressing it and an option for download video should appear. If the video is hosted in different resolutions, give me options to download the different resolutions. Name the resolutions and their respective file size next to them. Figure out how to allow video downloads for as many sources as possible, even websites that don't allow downloads. The video downloader app can do this. Figure out how it does it." (research/VIDEO_DOWNLOADER.md = how such apps work: network sniffing of media requests + page scan + HLS/DASH manifests; long-press on a video -> "Download video" -> quality sheet "1080p - 48 MB"; direct mp4/webm, HLS (TS/fMP4, AES-128), DASH (video+audio merged); not DRM) **P0+P1 DONE + EMULATOR-VERIFIED (alpha11)**: research/VIDEO_DOWNLOADER.md; sniffer in the extension -> Tab.media -> long-press menu + Menu tile + sheet with quality/size rows; plain files and HLS (TS, AES-128, fMP4) download and save (output byte-identical to the segments on the emulator). NOT yet: DASH (detected, says so), TS->MP4 conversion (saved as .ts), real-site testing (the owner's tablet), MP4 header probe for naming bare files. Honest: DRM, YouTube, live streams, expired links cannot be saved. **alpha12 adds: DASH (MPD parser, picture + sound merged into one MP4 with MediaExtractor/MediaMuxer, verified on the emulator with an ffmpeg DASH stream), TS streams converted to MP4 (ADTS->raw AAC frames; verified: h264+aac, decodes clean), HLS with separate audio merged the same way, MP4 header probe names the resolution of bare files (reads the first ~256 KB when the sheet opens), media attributed to the tab on the exact page.** [shipped kestrel-g-v0.2.0-alpha11, BUILDLOG: Download video]
- [x] V5 "Enable parallel downloading where available and download threading and other technologies and methods for fast download speeds. Make sure the downloads can be safely paused and resumed." (segments in parallel for files and for HLS/DASH segment lists, adaptive connection count, large buffers, few DB writes, HTTP/2 where the server offers it, resume of every kind of job after pause/kill) **MOSTLY DONE (alpha11)**: parallel Range segments for files (existing engine), parallel segment workers for streams, pause/resume proven on the emulator (no traffic while paused, resumes where it stopped, survives a server restart); JVM tests for kill-and-resume (StreamEngineTest). Tuning of connection counts on the real tablet still open. [shipped kestrel-g-v0.2.0-alpha11/12, BUILDLOG: parallel segment engine, resumable]
- [x] V6 "The app's subtle neon pulsing animations still aren't showing." (the owner has alpha8/9: the emulator shows the pulse with system animations off, so something on the tablet still holds it: candidates battery saver, the 2 minute rest, the finger-down hold; make the pulse nearly unconditional, show its state live in Settings, add a 10 s "Test the pulse") **CODE DONE (alpha9/10), EMULATOR ONLY**: neon holds cut to keyboard-up / first 3 s of load / finger drag / app away / covered by a full-screen page; Settings > Appearance shows 'The glow right now' (why it is holding) + 'Test the pulse (10 s)'. Needs the owner's tablet to confirm. [shipped kestrel-g-v0.2.0-alpha7..alpha10, BUILDLOG: the pulse is visible even with system animations off, Settings says why it holds; the owner did not mention it again after alpha10]
- [x] V7 "Review the app code and logic and improve speed, page loading, and stuttering as much as possible" (code review of the hot paths with findings fixed; D1 in-extension blocking engine = no per-request round trip; measure what can be measured; say what only the tablet can show) **PART DONE (alpha12)**: two read-only reviews saved in research/REVIEW-V7.md with a status per finding. Done: download notification/UI refresh storm (the biggest stutter source after alpha10's per-second refresh), per-touch engine call, tab grid rebuild coalescing, keyboard probe rate, suggestion debounce, Downloads screen binding, progress-line steps, PullChip relayout; page load: the extension no longer restarts at every launch (setAllowedInPrivateBrowsing guard), reply completed off the UI thread, verdict cache cleared per page load + batched counters, cosmetics on their own thread, release strips Log.d/v. **alpha13**: D1 (above) done in code; element-hiding CSS: only the top document gets the generic set (iframes get their site's rules); PSL parse skips IDN conversion for ASCII lines; the first page after a launch is judged from the extension's saved list copy instead of going unfiltered while the app's lists load. (research/SCROLL.md = causes ranked by evidence; fixes in code; measurement the owner can run; nothing here is verified on the tablet until the owner says so)
  - [x] Q2a (code done, EMULATOR-VERIFIED 2026-11-11: content process killed with `kill -9` after scrolling a long page -> dimmed preview + "Page freed to save memory - bringing it back" -> the page returned at the SAME scroll position; Diagnostics counters gecko.kill / gecko.recover.state; not seen on the tablet) white screen + reload = the content process was killed/crashed: today `sessionGone()` drops the saved SessionState and does a plain `loadUri` (page restarts at the top). Restore the SessionState (history + scroll position) first, show the tab's last preview instead of bare white until the first paint, count + log every kill/crash with free-RAM and trim level so the owner's Diagnostics screenshot shows the cause
  - [x] Q2b memory (research/SCROLL.md, an agent read the Firefox 160 sources; it CORRECTED my belief of a ~30 MB cap): the decoded-image cache is RAM / `image.mem.surfacecache.size_factor` (default 4 = about 475 MB on this tablet) and `max_size_kb` is forced to 1 GB on a 32-bit engine, so the `max_size_kb = 98304` line of alpha3-5 did nothing. Every `low-memory` event (Android trim 15) flushes ALL unlocked decoded images + WebRender's caches + the back-forward cache, and GeckoView has no switch to soften that. => gecko-prefs.txt v2: `image.mem.surfacecache.size_factor = 20` (about 95 MB held, so less RAM -> fewer events); Diagnostics reads the values back (emulator: version=2 size_factor=20). Effect on the tablet UNMEASURED.
  - [x] Q2c GPU / decoding (research): nothing needs switching on - WebRender, the GPU process, APZ and MediaCodec are the Android defaults; MSE VP9 is already off on Android so YouTube-class sites get H.264 = hardware MediaCodec; image decode is CPU-only in Gecko (no hardware image decode exists); ffvpx cannot be disabled (no pref in 160) and carries MP3/Opus/Vorbis/FLAC. Diagnostics now shows the GPU strings read through EGL (vendor / renderer / GL version = Mali driver build) and the gpu-process / software-WebRender prefs. The tablet's real GPU/driver is still UNREAD.

MEASURED (emulator = software GL, so only RELATIVE numbers mean anything): 60 Hz RenderThread animation 199 frames per 10 s; stepped 10 Hz 94 frames per 10 s (RenderThread 36 % of a core on the emulator while pulsing); 0 frames per 10 s once resting. - [ ] V3b (own idea, from the V3 emulator run) a DOWNLOAD-ONLY process: after a swipe-away with a download running the whole Gecko engine (main ~280 MB + content + GPU + helper on the emulator) stays alive only to host the download engine. Design: the engine + DownloadService live in a second process (`android:process=":dl"`), the UI process is a client over Messenger/AIDL snapshots; swipe-away then kills the browser and its helpers at once and leaves a ~40 MB downloader. Cost: IPC for views/commands, page downloads that use the Gecko fetcher (kind 1: cookies) would need the cookie/Referer/UA captured at enqueue time (the video downloader sniffer captures them anyway), update download and notifications must work from :dl. Do after the video downloader (V4/V5) because it reuses its header capture.

## Owner request 9 (INBOX 2026-11-12, verbatim in INBOX.md; kept in the owner's words)
- [x] X1 "Alpha9 says it is up to date" / "The in app updater for the last couple versions says the app is up to date even when a new version was released" - FIND THE ROOT CAUSE (suspects: version-name ordering "alpha9" > "alpha10", prerelease handling of the Releases API, ETag/304 handling, tag prefix) and fix it with regression tests; tell the owner how to get from the broken version to the fixed one. **DONE (alpha14, tests; rescue verified after publishing)**: ROOT CAUSE = GitHub lists this repository's releases sorted by TAG NAME (alpha9, alpha8 ... alpha2, alpha13, alpha12, alpha11, alpha10), the updater read only the first 8 entries (`per_page=8`), so alpha10 and later were never in its window and every version said 'up to date' (observed with `list_releases` page 1/2 on 2026-11-12). Fix: `UpdateLogic.compareTags/newestTags/tagsFromRefs` (natural order: alpha10 > alpha9), `UpdateManager.fetchReleases` = tag list (`git/matching-refs/tags/<prefix>`) + one conditional request per newest tag (ETag/304, headers only when nothing changed), fallback `releases?per_page=100`; the offer is recomputed from the stored bodies against the code installed NOW. Existing installs (alpha9-13 all have the old 8-entry window) are rescued by the tag `kestrel-g-v0.3.0-alpha14`: '0.3.0' sorts above every '0.2.0-alphaN', so it lands inside their window. Tests: `UpdateLogicTest` (real tag order, old window blind to alpha13, rescue tag visible), `UpdateCheckTest` (end-to-end against a GitHub-like local server: finds alpha13 from alpha9, second look = 304s, recomputes after a manual install).
- [x] X2 "Make the app check once per day if there are any new versions of itself, of the browser engine it uses, and any ad blocking updates. If any updates are available, send me a dialog to let me know and an update button, then download and update the app in the background." - SHIPPED alpha16 (kestrel-g-v0.3.0-alpha16, run 43143728373 green): core UpdatePlan/EngineWatch + tests, UpdateManager.checkEngine (ETag, UpdateCheckTest), BrowserActivity.maybeShowUpdateNotice dialog (Update/Later/Details; Install now when ready; once per news key), autoUpdateDownload default OFF, ad-block lists refresh every day (UpdateJob, Settings labels, 90 s front fallback). EMULATOR: dialog on first start, Update -> background download -> "Update ready to install" dialog -> Install now -> Updates sheet "Android needs your OK first"
- [~] X3 "Turn the download manager into a full fledged download manager optimized for maximum speed, multiple downloads, good pause and resume logic that doesn't break or corrupt files, parallel downloading and threading, etc. Research the technology and methods of popular and powerful download managers to implement." - IN PROGRESS (shipped in alpha16: M2, M3, M4, fast start, M1 compare, M7 sanity): research/DOWNLOAD_MANAGERS.md read; DONE+tested in EngineTest/PlannerTest/StreamEngineTest: M4 auto-resume after a kill (Engine/StreamEngine.resumeInterrupted, crashes cap 2, SQLite v3), M3 a refused extra connection retires alone + HillClimb.refused (10 min hold), M2 outage ladder (WAITING_NETWORK with growing probes, STALLED after 30 min, fresh download fails fast), M5-lite two connections at once for files >= 8 MiB. ALSO DONE (alpha17 candidates, tested): M1 Last-Modified compare + validator-less 4 KiB tail compare (Sink.pread), M7 content sanity (web page where a file was expected, HLS segment sanity). ALSO DONE (in tree, ships with the next alpha): M8-lite Settings > Downloads (speed limit, downloads at the same time, connections per download; RateLimiter + tests). STILL OPEN from the research: M5 rest (bounded probe window for HttpURLConnection, slow-connection recycle), M6 digest, M8 priorities/reorder, M9 IO path, M10 mirror flip, T0 raw-socket fake server
- [~] X4 "Make the app open as fast as possible, load web pages as fast as possible, and make everything snappy and the scrolling smooth and fast." - PARTLY (alpha16): measurement first - Diagnostics 'Page loads' (host, first paint, done, blocked, visit #), Network lab switches (DoH, HTTP/3) and 'Warm up the top suggestion' (preconnect, alpha17), cheaper UI refresh, lists refresh 90 s after start. Real speed work needs the OWNER's numbers (research/AUDIT-2026-11.md section 3)
- [~] X5 "Figure out if it is using the best ad and tracker blocking software and technology, and make it auto update the ad blocking and implement the best available ad blocking and tracker blocking technology." - ASSESSED (research/AUDIT-2026-11.md section 5, research/ADBLOCK_ENGINE.md): own engine kept, lists refresh EVERY DAY (alpha16); pop-up/redirect guard added (alpha15). Figure out a way for the browser to reject these redirects and spam tabs." - DONE alpha15: three-layer guard (guard-main.js MAIN world + JumpGuard + refusePopup): web-tests/guard.test.js 15 cases, JumpGuardTest, emulator popups.html (window.open ad, hidden link, redirect, timer BLOCKED; same-site window.open and real link OPEN; Diagnostics counters guard.open/click/redirect); owner-side check on real movie sites pending

## Owner message 33 (2026-11-13T23:23Z): a research question the owner sent to the OTHER Claude account (read-only: "without writing to the repo"); answered there
- [x] AS0 "research If it is possible to optimize the underlying gecko browser engine for the hardware I have, such as removing unnecessary elements, shrinking the size, making the engine more efficient for my hardware" - answered by the other account at 2026-11-13T23:49Z (its reply is in OUTBOX.md, merged into this branch by sync): rebuilding Gecko is not worth it (the shipped build is already PGO + LTO + -O2 + "lite"; a rebuild would trim 5-10 MB of 118 MB and needs a rented 16-32 core machine every release); the real saving is how the engine is STORED: keep the libraries uncompressed inside the APK (extractNativeLibs=false, geckoapp/build.gradle.kts about line 68) = installed size 230 MB -> about 158 MB (73 MB less) for a +65 MB bigger update download; GeckoView has supported in-APK libraries since 96 (Firefox for Android stopped forcing extraction in 143), so the repo's note "GeckoView needs the libraries on disk" (research/UPDATE_STORAGE.md) looks wrong but is UNTESTED; omni.ja trim about 9 MB more; the :crashhelper process cannot be switched off (Gecko 160 waits for it); settings are near their ceiling; first measure per-process memory (Diagnostics). Nothing was run on a tablet or emulator.
- [ ] AS1 stored libraries (extractNativeLibs=false): test on the emulator (start, page load, in-place update, engine start time) and then on the tablet, ONLY after the owner says yes: it trades +65 MB of download over the hotspot for 73 MB of storage, and storage is what blocked the owner's updates before. Ask in the next answer.
- [ ] AS2 (my follow-up to AS0's advice "first measure per-process memory", not the owner's words; after alpha33): one Diagnostics line "Memory by process (PSS, MB)" for the app's own processes (parent, tab, gpu, socket, utility, crashhelper) + device free memory, read when the report is built (not on a timer: ActivityManager.getProcessMemoryInfo is slow), same short names as CpuMeter, unit test for the formatting, emulator look at the line; then the owner's Diagnostics paste says which process is worth trimming (GPU process off/on is already a pref; do NOT touch :crashhelper).
- NOTE for every session: `INBOX.md` lines dated about 2026-11-13T23:23Z to 23:50Z that came in by the sync merge of branch `claude/dazzling-bohr-qjnxhe` (about 480 lines) are that account's own hook captures (its helper agents' reports), not owner requests; the only owner message among them is AS0 above, already answered.

## Owner message 42 (2026-11-15, verbatim in INBOX.md): tapping YouTube's seek bar must jump the video, and buffering must start there
- [ ] YS1 (owner message 42) "I'm on YouTube and the videos have a red seek bar. The red line is the portion of the video already watched. The red dot is the current part in the video I'm watching. The rest of the line is a light color to the right side of the red dot for unwatched video. When I press anywhere on this bar on a regular browser or in YouTube app, it will jump the video to the place I pressed on the bar (the red dot will jump to where I pressed). But in Kestrel, it doesn't jump the video at all, it just makes a strange light colored line up to the point I pressed. I want the seek bar for videos inside the browser to act like other browsers and video player apps-- if I press a spot on the video player bar, it should jump the video to that part of the bar. It should start buffering video from that point forward and not buffer anything or waste any internet on any part of the video prior to the part I am currently watching"

## Second independent review of the video bar scripts (vfind.js / videobar.js / content.js long press, 2026-11-15; repros t1..t10 were in the reviewer's scratch folder): fix before alpha49
All twelve are coded and tested (CODED + proven by web-tests/videobar.test.js 25 of 25 and npm test 443 pass, 2026-11-15): every finding has its own test (RW1 .. RW11 in videobar.test.js; RW12 is cleanup: the video menu is sent by the handlers through VF.srcOf, the bar's debug hooks stay for the tests only, 'shown' is reported only when the bar is drawn).
- [x] RW1 (MAJOR) a video that is NOT in the elementsFromPoint stack counts as "under the finger" by rectangle alone: a link / button / picture over a decorative video gave the Save sheet (long press) or Kestrel's bar (tap). Fix: such a video counts only when the top-most stack element is a bare layer (no a[href], button, field, role, onclick, pointer cursor; at most ~24 characters of text); taps whose top-most element is interactive are ignored
- [x] RW2 (MAJOR) full screen of a shadow-host box left no bar (the bar sat in the light DOM of a host with no slot, 0x0): the bar's home is `VF.rootOf(fe) || fe` in show() and onFullscreen()
- [x] RW3 (MAJOR) ownControls only looked at the first 1500 candidates of the document: look in the player's box (a few levels up from the film) first, the document only as the fallback
- [x] RW4 (MOD) panel and Save sheet could both open when the frame round trip took more than 400 ms: the top frame answers {video:true, src}, the frame itself sends vmenu only if the panel was not already sent
- [x] RW5 (MOD) the tap window (600 ms) was longer than the engine's long press (500 ms): a press released at 500-600 ms was a menu AND a tap (bar behind the sheet): tap = 450 ms, and a contextmenu stamps a time that makes tapped() ignore the release
- [x] RW6 (MOD) frame relay matched by size only: two frames of one size gave the bar / menu to the wrong film: more than one frame of that size = no video
- [x] RW7 (MOD) the page could set data-kestrel-bar on its own layer to switch the long press menu / panel off, and fake pointerdown / pointercancel events cancelled real taps: the bar is recognised by identity (KestrelVB.isBar), the attribute is gone, untrusted events return first
- [x] RW8 (MINOR) the touch path: the pre-check at pointerdown ran VF.at + ownControls synchronously on every touch near a video; frames scanned the whole document twice per tap: ~60 ms timer cleared by pointercancel / pointermove, all() cached for 2 s
- [x] RW9 (MINOR) no bar for a second film while the first film's bar is up: hide the old one and go on
- [x] RW10 (MINOR) the late look (1.7 s) could flip a good player that auto-hides its controls: remember 'own' for that film and skip the late look
- [x] RW11 (MINOR) a failed guard answer meant Off for the page's whole life: answer the waiters but do not store it
- [x] RW12 (MINOR) cleanup: content.js uses VF.menu, api.dbg / api.hide not in release builds' reach, report('shown') only when the bar is really drawn, vfind.js listed once per script group

Found in the screenshots (pixel rows measured): both system bars stay VISIBLE in full screen on the tablet (status bar 0-26 px, navigation bar 52 px at the bottom / 972-1024 in portrait), and the page is laid out FULL-BLEED under them (the film sits centred on the whole 1024 px screen): alpha48's FS1 zeroes the root padding while a page is full screen and API 29 also sets LAYOUT_* flags, which is right only where the system really hides the bars (the emulator does; this ROM evidently does not, or not always).

## Owner message 47 (2026-11-15, verbatim in INBOX.md): alpha50 changed nothing on the tablet
Owner words: "I haven't noticed any change with the new version. The controls are still under Android navigation bar. There are no controls in full screen. The video didn't pause when switching orientation. It looks like nothing changed. When switching orientation, it is laggy and for a few seconds the video is the wrong size in either mode. Make it pause the video and properly set up the size and orientation smoothly then resume. Make the controls available for both orientations and never underneath the Android navigation bar"
Reading: alpha48, alpha49 and alpha50 look the same on the tablet, so the cause is not the layout flags or the insets listener (alpha49/50 removed both). Working theory: this ROM's window manager honours HIDE_NAVIGATION / FULLSCREEN by laying the window out over the whole screen, while its System UI keeps drawing both bars on top; nothing the app can read says so (the insets say the bars are gone). The only robust fix is to stop asking for hidden bars by default and keep the page between the bars (the same layout the tablet uses for normal browsing, where the dock is never under the bar). The muted video of the Yahoo page opens no engine media session, so the app-side pause (alpha49) never touched it.
- [~] FB1 (CODED 2026-11-15, guard test aFullScreenPageKeepsTheSystemBarsAndStaysBetweenThemUnlessThePersonChoseHide; lab proof pending: suite fsy keep vs hide) (owner msg 47 points 1 + 3) full screen keeps the system bars and lays the page out between them (no immersive request) by default; Settings row "System bars in full-screen video": Keep (default) / Hide; the player's controls and Kestrel's bar are in view in both orientations; guard test + lab (suite fsy: strip visible above the navigation bar in the default mode)
- [x] FB2 (CODED + web-tests/turn.test.js 9 of 9, npm test 452 pass, guard test; lab proof of the live Yahoo player pending) (owner msg 47 point 2) the video is paused for every turn whether or not it makes sound: a page-level guard (`turn.js`, every frame) pauses the playing videos when the screen turns, and plays them again once the viewport has been still for 650 ms and nobody touched the screen; tests in web-tests/turn.test.js
- [~] FB3 (CODED: one extra resize after the turn settled, test 'the extra resize comes once the viewport is still'; the stale-size symptom on the live Yahoo player is not yet reproduced anywhere) (owner msg 47 "for a few seconds the video is the wrong size in either mode") after a turn the page is given one more `resize` once the viewport is still (a page that sized its player from a stale viewport redoes it), the black cover of the app stays until then; proof on the live Yahoo player in the lab (pictures yahoofs-*) or, if the lab cannot show it, a Diagnostics line that records the viewport sizes the page saw after each turn (so the next report says what happened)
- [~] ANS47 (ANSWER GIVEN 2026-11-15 21:40Z with the alpha52 Release link kestrel-g-v0.3.0-alpha52 (green run 43763812046, recorded in BUILDLOG); the follow-up with the lab verdict of FB4 (run 43763822027) is still owed) answer owner message 47 in plain words, with the alpha51 Release link, what changed and what was NOT run on the tablet

### Owner message 48 (2026-11-15 ~21:40Z, two screenshots taken 16:37:40 and 16:37:47 local; the pictures stay out of the repo, described here in words; rows measured with a small script)
Owner: "The attached screenshots are what happens when I rotate between portrait and landscape. The video size and frame is awkward for a few seconds"
Reading (Yahoo Finance full-screen player, collapse icon at its top left, the bars are KEPT: status bar 0-26, navigation bar at the bottom, so the app is alpha51 or newer): (1) PORTRAIT 600 x 1024 view 26-972: the page is still laid out for the LANDSCAPE window: the film is 16:9 at the landscape height (522 px high, y 26-548, about 928 px wide, centred in a 1024 px wide box: a 47 px black letterbox strip at the left and the picture cut off at the right screen edge), the page background fills y 549-972 under it; (2) LANDSCAPE 1024 x 600 view 26-548: the page is still laid out for the PORTRAIT window: the player box is 600 px wide (x 0-599), the film (600 x 338) sits at y 331 = exactly the portrait centring in a 947 px box, cut by the navigation bar at y 548, and the page background fills x 600-1023. So: the ENGINE's page viewport keeps the previous orientation's size for a few seconds after the turn while the view is already right (the page had NOT been told yet: no resize event, so the page-side report of FB4 cannot fire either); the black cover of ROT1 lifted when the app's own view stopped changing (650 ms, cap 2.2 s), which is before the engine catches up on this slow tablet (the emulator catches up within the settle time, so the lab never showed it).
- [~] ANS48 (ANSWER GIVEN 2026-11-15 22:45Z with the alpha53 Release link kestrel-g-v0.3.0-alpha53 (green run 43770059374, recorded in BUILDLOG): what the screenshots showed, why, what changed, what was NOT run on the tablet, what to send back; stays open until the owner's tablet test of alpha53 comes back with the Diagnostics lines 'Turning under a video', 'Full-screen page size', 'Full-screen system bars')

- [~] ROT3 (my own finding in lab run 43763822027 (alpha52 code), 2026-11-15; probably part of "laggy" on the tablet too) the FIRST manual turn after a full screen began made the screen turn TWICE: Kestrel handed its one-time sideways turn back to the tablet ("follow the tablet") the instant the raw orientation sensor showed the new position, while Android's own rotation sensing still believed in the old one, so the screen went to PORTRAIT first (Health `life config orientation=portrait`) and to landscape 0.45 s later: two engine re-layouts, the slowest turn of the run (1554 ms). CODED for alpha54: `FullscreenTurner.moved` waits RELEASE_SETTLE_MS (650 ms) of the tablet staying in that position before it hands the screen back (a turn back meanwhile cancels), guard test aOneTimeTurnIsHandedBackOnlyAfterTheTabletHasStayedInItsPositionForAMoment, lab suite fsy counts the config changes of every state ("[N config changes]", issue when 2 or more); lab proof pending (fsy run after the compat run)

### Owner message 49 (2026-11-15 ~23:00Z): "This video player needs an audit and overhaul"
Owner, verbatim: "On alpha50, the controls showed up in portrait mode as usual at the very bottom and the Android navigation bar hid. But if I pull up the navigation bar, it covers the video controls. If I go to full screen and rotate the screen, there are no video controls or seek bar for landscape mode at all. If I rotate back and forth from landscape to portrait, the video sizing glitches for a few seconds and I don't think it pauses but I could be wrong / Also when I switch to landscape full screen (which has no player controls) then switch back to portrait, portrait mode then no longer has player controls / On alpha51, the video player first falsely reported that the video was 4 seconds long when it was over one minute. When I first opened full screen it stretched the video to fill the screen which messed up the aspect ratio. But when I went to landscape mode then back, it turned to its normal aspect ratio. The rotation from portrait to landscape and back is buggy and slow. The video was slow and laggy. The controls now show up in both orientations. When the controls are showing and I tap the video, the controls should disappear and dedicate the whole screen to the video, just like other video plays. This video player needs an audit and overhaul. Do whatever research necessary such as reviewing the logic and coding and behavior of other well known and well coded browser video players. Do a significant overhaul and make the video player run efficiently and smoothly with controls easily accessible but each time I tap the screen it should toggle playback controls shown or hidden, just like other popular video players."
Facts the message gives (not guesses): on alpha50 the owner's navigation bar DID hide in full screen (immersive works on this tablet; my "the ROM keeps its bars" theory was wrong for portrait) and pulling it up covered the controls; alpha51 shows controls in both orientations (the Keep setting works); alpha51 FS entry stretched the picture (wrong aspect) until a turn and back; the duration read 4 s at first (an ad or a growing MSE duration); the bar does not hide on a tap (reading of videobar.js: a tap on a film whose bar is up only RESTARTS its 4 s timer, `judge()` -> `keep()`: it never toggles).

FIX (coded, 15 page tests ok incl. a mutation check): gate-main.js `nativePlay` on each tracked element's trusted `play` event (a closed element with controls = the person's press: open + ask 'press'; an open element that was handed over before + a tap behind it = pressed again: ask again; a play() the page made itself (viaPlay) is the replacement's business). The ho scene now also logs which tap events the page saw and presses `file` a second time after Back. RUN 2 = 43831112200 (head bbfc9c3f): the plain video (file) and the broken address (bad) now hand over / fall back correctly on the real engine, overlay + hls + unknown + setting never + Diagnostics ok; the remaining red checks were lab-driver faults (the page's timers stand still while Kestrel's player is in front: judged after Back now; the second press must tap the engine's bar button). RUN 3 = vp 43832408756 (head 6b32808a) pending, then ho again on the head with the tap check (ANS54e.9) -- PROVEN on the real engine: lab vp 43838527885 (hand-over scene 29 checks ok) + Release alpha58

  RE-SENT 2026-11-16T03:30Z (the first paragraph again, without the research question; the worker of the session was restarted while the first copy was being answered): not a new request, covered by the rows below; the answer to it is the same answer as to message 52 (list the open rows again at its end).
  Reading: (1) the stuck status-bar icons and the cropped video are the SAME symptom: for a moment the screen shows the OLD portrait buffers (600 px wide) through the new landscape display (1024 wide): the system keeps old frames until the app window has drawn its first frame at the new size, and our UI thread is blocked in GeckoView's synchronous surface / compositor resize (Health: `ui slow 887 ms`, `ui late-beat 2021 ms` right after `gecko size 1024x523`); (2) the "very laggy video" has three suspects that the new alpha56 numbers will separate (the page's script thread starved by the page's own scripts and memory trims, 1080p decode, GPU composition of the video through the engine); (3) the question about other browsers and third-party code is a research task whose answer goes into research/ files.

## Independent review of the alpha52 + alpha53 change set (2026-11-15, agent a4b6a0705a3019258, read-only): 1 MAJOR + 8 MINOR, fixed for alpha54
- [x] RV53-1 (MAJOR, FIXED + tests) on a single-page site (finance.yahoo.com, m.youtube.com) the page's viewport report was credited to NO tab (the address the extension knows is the one the page was loaded with, the tab's address follows pushState, and the report is cut to 300 characters), `vpOk` stayed true from the hello, and EVERY turn waited the whole 7 s: `tabOfReport` now ends with the full-screen / front tab of the same HOST, compares 300 characters on both sides, `pageStarted` clears `vpOk`, and a page that has said NOTHING 3 s into a turn is let go by the old rule (TurnSettle.SILENT_MS)
- [x] RV53-2 (turn.js) `landscape` is refreshed when a turn finishes (a turn and back inside one window made the next keyboard resize look like a flip): web test added
- [x] RV53-3 a second turn while one goes on starts its own clocks (a rotation at 6.9 s got 0.1 s of cover; a touch counted from the first turn): `configChanged` restarts `startedAt`, test added
- [x] RV53-4 a touch that ends the wait is no longer booked as "the page gave up" (text "ended by a touch ..."), and the black cover now TAKES the touch (the tap must not reach the page nobody can see)
- [x] RV53-5 a failed `configChanged` setup calls `abandon()` (the cover can never stay up with no timer)
- [x] RV53-6 no futile 1 px size change when both axes are off by more than a tenth (desktop mode, a page with no viewport meta): the display race of FB4 is one axis
- [x] RV53-8 a private tab's page keeps no counter and no log line of its viewport reports
- [x] RV53-9 tests: the manifest order guard really checks the order, the sizeChanged guard names the one-liner, the hello web test waits for the message instead of fixed sleeps

### Found by the first CLEAN emulator run of suite fsy (lab run 43760546128, alpha51 code, 2026-11-15; research/FULLSCREEN_BARS.md "Addendum B")
Real engine, Keep mode: sound stops when the app is left (page clock frozen 0.0 s, engine session paused, all four cases); every turn: app pause + cover + settle in 720-1085 ms with ONE view size change (600x947 <-> 973x574, i.e. exactly between the bars: the side navigation bar in landscape) and the page's own one extra `resize` 0.72 s later (turn.js works); the red strip is visible right above the navigation bar in every state EXCEPT the first state after the first full-screen entry of the run: there the lab page reported `innerHeight` 956 CSS px = 1024 px (the DISPLAY height) while the view is 947 px, and it stayed so until the first turn: the engine sizes a page that goes full screen to the display and does not re-fit it to the view until the view changes size again (a timing race between the engine's own full-screen resize and the app's relayout: the other entries were right). That cuts off a player's bottom controls even with the bars kept, and fits the owner's third screenshot (box 575 px = the old landscape height after turning back).
- [x] FB4 (DONE 2026-11-15, shipped in alpha52; REAL-ENGINE EVIDENCE lab run 43763822027: at the first entry the page reported 600x947 = the view (right, no correction needed that time: the display-height race of run 43760546128 is intermittent) and after a turn to landscape the page still reported 600x947 while the view was 973x574: 'fullscreen viewport mismatch ... the engine is given a real size change (try 1)' and 0.6 s later 'right: page 973x574, view 973x574'; Diagnostics line 'Full-screen page size: 28 checks, 1 wrong, 1 size change given'; earlier status text: CODED 2026-11-15 21:2xZ for alpha52: web-tests/turn.test.js 10 of 10, npm test 453 pass, :core/:app/:geckoapp unit suites green, guard test OwnerRulesTest.aFullScreenPageThatGotTheWrongViewportIsGivenARealSizeChange; LAB PROOF PENDING: suite fsy state 'began' of the first variant must show the red strip) (owner msg 47 points 1 + 3 + "wrong size for a few seconds") the page tells the app the viewport it really got (turn.js `vpt`, top frame, in full screen, 350 ms after each resize / fullscreenchange); when it is not the view's size the app gives the engine a real size change (the view 1 px shorter for 120 ms) up to 3 times and logs `fullscreen viewport ...` lines + a Diagnostics line; proof: lab suite fsy state 'began' of the first variant shows the red strip; web test for the report; guard test

## Independent review of the alpha49 Kotlin change set (2026-11-15, agent a1b60178ab815123f; javap of GeckoView 160 MediaSession checked): fix before alpha50
- [x] RA1 (MAJOR, CODED + tests 2026-11-15) a closed / hibernated / replaced session left `GeckoTabData.videoPlaying` true and the `MediaSession` object in place (no "deactivated" ever comes): a turn then covered a page with no video, "paused" a dead session (a silent no-op in GeckoView) and counted it, silenceStrays counted a stray pause on every run, and the dead session stayed reachable: the `Tab.session` setter forgets both when the session is cleared or replaced; both consumers also check `session != null`
- [x] RA2 (MAJOR, CODED + guard test 2026-11-15; lab proof pending: suite fsy with nohide must print KEPT) on Android 10 the decor pads itself and consumes the insets before the root's listener, so the "system bars" note read zero while both bars were on screen: `checkBars` now reads `window.decorView.rootWindowInsets` (`systemBarsNow`); the layout itself was right (the decor does the padding once the LAYOUT flags are gone)
- [x] RA3 (minor, CODED) onDestroy cancels the stray / bars / re-hide timers and the turn (a named Runnable replaces the lambda); the dead `settledCallback` is gone
- [x] RA4 (minor, CODED + 12 tests 2026-11-15) the turn state machine had no test: `core/media/TurnSettleMachine.kt` (pure, fake host and clock) + `TurnSettleMachineTest` (first configuration, ordinary page, pause + cover + resume, second turn extends the first (found by the test: it used to be ignored once the video was paused), the cap, a touch, the person pressing play meanwhile, another tab / app away, abandon, a silent video, a failed pause); `web/TurnSettler.kt` is a thin host

The owner updates every day or two and a sideloaded build otherwise runs interpreted until Android's idle-and-charging dexopt. Work: (1) the release workflow publishes the `.dm` next to the APK; (2) UpdateManager.install() writes it into the session as `kestrel-update.dm` (verify its size/hash like the APK), (3) retries the session WITHOUT it on INSTALL_FAILED_BAD_DEX_METADATA, (4) Robolectric tests for the fallback, (5) the `aot` lab suite's dm_install_check re-run on the new build, (6) a tablet test (Android Go may not honour it: nothing lost). It only helps updates FROM the build that carries the new updater, so it needs its own release.
